Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- NeMo-Guardrails is a Kubernetes component.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 1337, 5000, 8000.
- connecting to external services\: aiohttp, httpx, openai, redis, sqlalchemy, urllib.
- runtime dependencies\: Redis.
- 141 Python package dependencies (141 required), categories\: 1 grpc, 1 image-processing, 1 llm-sdk, 1 tokenizer, 1 web-server, 18 observability, 2 ml-framework, 2 numerical, 2 serialization, 3 web-framework.
- Python k8s client with 66 API call(s).
